\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 3. Revision Scope and Preservation Rules\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 This revision covers wording, evidence requirements, claim-scope rules, report guidance, exclusion language and discipline-specific guardrails for Acting inside the current shared TapeCoach architecture.\
The baseline text actually revised is the supplied 0B Acting rubric slice, with Acting-specific additions to the 0E guardrail logic. Because the supplied baseline does not contain detailed existing blocks for every required topic, several additions are presented as new Acting addenda rather than pretending they already existed.\
This revision does not cover implementation, code, database schema, report schema, public JSON structure, score calculation, backend field names, UI rendering, comparison-page labels, frontend helper logic or live-output QA.\
The following must remain unchanged:\

\cf0 Proposed revised text\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 In spoken-only Acting and Monologue reports, the stored vocal field may continue to operate internally as the speech-delivery slot, but user-facing language must not present that category as singing, vocal performance or song-based voice unless singing is actually present. In acting-only outputs, interpret this area as voice and speech delivery: audibility, clarity, diction, articulation, modulation, pace, pitch, volume and how speech serves the text, character or screen/stage task.\cell \row

\clvertalc \clshdrawnil \clwWidth13867\clftsWidth3 \clmart10 \clmarl10 \clmarb10 \clmarr10 \clbrdrt\brdrnil \clbrdrl\brdrnil \clbrdrb\brdrnil \clbrdrr\brdrnil \clpadt20 \clpadl20 \clpadb20 \clpadr20 \gaph\cellx8640
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Proposed revised text\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Acting feedback must use the most specific form or medium supported by the brief, task or tape: monologue, scene/duologue, screen/self-tape, stage, classical/heightened text, contemporary text, or mixed acting task. If the form is unclear, state that and keep the language to observable shared acting evidence. Shared domains include text handling, intention, listening/response, thought shifts, stakes, rhythm/pacing, speech delivery, physical choices, camera or reader relationship where relevant, and suitability to any supplied brief. Do not treat all Acting tapes as one undifferentiated acting standard.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Current baseline excerpt actually being revised\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 \'93Feedback should point to observable beats, objectives, transitions, reaction moments, stakes and clarity of thought.\'94\cell \row

\itap1\trowd \taflags0 \trgaph108\trleft-108 \trbrdrl\brdrnil \trbrdrr\brdrnil 
\clvertalc \clshdrawnil \clwWidth1612\clftsWidth3 \clmart10 \clmarl10 \clmarb10 \clmarr10 \clbrdrt\brdrnil \clbrdrl\brdrnil \clbrdrb\brdrnil \clbrdrr\brdrnil \clpadt20 \clpadl20 \clpadb20 \clpadr20 \gaph\cellx4320
\clvertalc \clshdrawnil \clwWidth13867\clftsWidth3 \clmart10 \clmarl10 \clmarb10 \clmarr10 \clbrdrt\brdrnil \clbrdrl\brdrnil \clbrdrb\brdrnil \clbrdrr\brdrnil \clpadt20 \clpadl20 \clpadb20 \clpadr20 \gaph\cellx8640
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Proposed revised text\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 In monologue work, evidence should be anchored in the structure of the speech: opening circumstance, shifts in thought, changes of action or address, stakes, rhythm, use of stillness or physical economy, speech clarity, and how the performer handles the text. Where the material is classical, Shakespearean, verse or heightened text, note text handling, sense, rhythm, imagery, thought progression and speech clarity only where they are observable. Where the material is contemporary, focus on text-specific choices, conversational rhythm, given circumstances where supplied, and behavioural clarity. Do not impose classical-text expectations on contemporary material or screen-naturalism expectations on stage-heightened material without source or brief support.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 In scene or duologue work, reader or scene-partner evidence must be treated as mediated tape evidence. It is valid to comment on the performer\'92s listening, response timing, eyeline, relationship target, thought shifts and use of the reader\'92s cues where those are observable. Reader availability, paid-reader quality, off-camera reader performance, or lack of an ideal reader must not be treated as the performer\'92s talent level. If the reader or cueing materially limits assessability, state that limitation rather than converting it into acting criticism.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Screen/self-tape Acting evidence must follow the actual task. Direct-to-camera tasks, ident/slate tasks, off-camera-reader scenes, monologues and read-in-line dialogue use different camera relationships. Do not apply one universal eyeline rule. Comment on eyeline, frame adaptation, non-verbal listening, stillness, physical economy, speech clarity, direct address and relation to the camera only where the task makes those relevant and the tape shows them.\cell \row

\clvertalc \clshdrawnil \clwWidth1612\clftsWidth3 \clmart10 \clmarl10 \clmarb10 \clmarr10 \clbrdrt\brdrnil \clbrdrl\brdrnil \clbrdrb\brdrnil \clbrdrr\brdrnil \clpadt20 \clpadl20 \clpadb20 \clpadr20 \gaph\cellx4320
\clvertalc \clshdrawnil \clwWidth13867\clftsWidth3 \clmart10 \clmarl10 \clmarb10 \clmarr10 \clbrdrt\brdrnil \clbrdrl\brdrnil \clbrdrb\brdrnil \clbrdrr\brdrnil \clpadt20 \clpadl20 \clpadb20 \clpadr20 \gaph\cellx8640
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Proposed revised text\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Acting self-tape assessment must separate technical assessability from acting quality. Stronger performance claims require readable sight, audible speech, task-appropriate framing, usable eyeline or reader placement, and enough continuity to judge the acting choice. Plain domestic capture, smartphone recording, simple backgrounds and limited props/costume may be fully acceptable if the acting evidence is readable. Do not reward expensive equipment, studio polish, elaborate sets, paid editing or cosmetic presentation as acting merit. If assessability is materially limited, narrow the claim and lower reliability rather than using generic criticism.\cell \row

\clvertalc \clshdrawnil \clwWidth13867\clftsWidth3 \clmart10 \clmarl10 \clmarb10 \clmarr10 \clbrdrt\brdrnil \clbrdrl\brdrnil \clbrdrb\brdrnil \clbrdrr\brdrnil \clpadt20 \clpadl20 \clpadb20 \clpadr20 \gaph\cellx8640
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Proposed revised text\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Separate speech audibility from speech performance. Audio language should describe whether the speech is audible enough to judge. Speech-delivery language may address diction, articulation, pace, pitch, volume, modulation, clarity of thought, response to text and suitability to the acting task only where the tape allows it. Do not infer weak speech skill from poor recording quality alone. Do not treat accent, speech difference or communication support as a deficit unless the brief explicitly requires a task-specific accent/dialect and the evidence is observable and safe.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Proposed revised text\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Treat response to direction, redirection, group work, workshop behaviour, interview readiness, training potential, broader flexibility and sustained professional process as live-room, admissions-specific or process-only evidence unless the tape directly shows them. A finished tape may show observable acting choices, but it should not be used to infer how the performer takes notes, collaborates, adapts over a workshop, behaves in a group room or develops across a training process unless that evidence is explicitly present.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Role, world, given circumstances, production style, objective or context claims must be grounded in supplied brief, text, slate, known material, or observable evidence. If no brief or material context is supplied, do not invent casting requirements, production world, role expectations, accent expectations, period expectations or hidden circumstances. Where a source or task separates performed extract from context questions, reflection or discussion, do not treat missing reflection evidence as a flaw in the extract unless the task required it.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Proposed revised text\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Acting assessment must separate access process, communication support and performance evidence. Access needs, visual impairment, neurodivergence, speech difference, mobility difference, interpreters, access workers, line-feeding, accessible scripts, additional preparation time, access riders/passports or adapted audition process must not be treated as acting deficits. Judge only what is shown and assessable in the task. Do not infer medical history, capability ceiling, professionalism, confidence or role suitability from access need, disability, speech difference, mobility difference or adaptation.\cell \row

\cf0 Acting feedback should remain methodology-neutral. It may discuss observable intention, action, listening, response, thought shift, physical choice, text handling, stakes, rhythm, camera relationship or speech delivery. It must not require or privilege Stanislavski, Meisner, Method, Brecht, Laban, Lecoq, Viewpoints, Gaulier, Commedia dell\'92Arte or any other named methodology unless the task or brief explicitly asks for it. Formal frameworks, exam-board domains, school preferences and course-shaped terminology may inform evidence language, but must not be imported as TapeCoach weights, score systems or universal method requirements.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Physical, embodied, fight, creative and comedy Acting comments must remain conditional and evidence-led. Physical/fight/creative acting evidence may be discussed where the tape or task directly includes physical storytelling, fight choreography, embodied performance, devised material, clowning or related form-specific work. Because the current evidence base is mainly specialist-course-shaped, do not present these as universal Acting criteria. Comedy evidence remains thin; do not create comedy-specific scoring claims beyond observable timing, rhythm, text handling, reaction, escalation or contrast where those are shown.\cell \row

\itap1\trowd \taflags0 \trgaph108\trleft-108 \trbrdrl\brdrnil \trbrdrr\brdrnil 
\clvertalc \clshdrawnil \clwWidth1613\clftsWidth3 \clmart10 \clmarl10 \clmarb10 \clmarr10 \clbrdrt\brdrnil \clbrdrl\brdrnil \clbrdrb\brdrnil \clbrdrr\brdrnil \clpadt20 \clpadl20 \clpadb20 \clpadr20 \gaph\cellx4320
\clvertalc \clshdrawnil \clwWidth13867\clftsWidth3 \clmart10 \clmarl10 \clmarb10 \clmarr10 \clbrdrt\brdrnil \clbrdrl\brdrnil \clbrdrb\brdrnil \clbrdrr\brdrnil \clpadt20 \clpadl20 \clpadb20 \clpadr20 \gaph\cellx8640
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Proposed revised text\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Every substantive Acting praise or criticism must include a behavioural evidence anchor. Generic phrases such as grounded, natural, believable, connected, good eyeline, strong choices, clear relationship, good pacing, emotionally truthful, strong presence, clear character, strong instincts, good voice, confident performance, professional self-tape, good energy or strong text work are not sufficient on their own. Replace them with what was visible, where it happened and why it mattered: a beat, line, thought shift, relationship target, response moment, camera relationship, speech choice, physical choice, text handling or assessability condition.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Proposed revised text\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Acting reports should prioritise timestamped behavioural evidence. For an assessable Acting or Monologue tape, timestamps should cover the beginning, middle and end where useful, and should include at least one high-value strength moment and one improvement moment where justified. Notes should identify the acting form, text moment, beat, thought shift, reader/camera relationship, speech choice, physical choice or assessability condition. Fix-first, strengths, improvements and next-take advice must connect to observable evidence. If assessability limits useful note density, state that limitation and lower reliability rather than padding the report with generic wording.\cell \row

\cf0 Acting reports should use these sections to show what was observable in this performer, this tape, this text, this role or character context where supplied, and this acting form or medium where identifiable.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b \cf0 Current discipline logic
\f1\b0 \
Acting is supported through acting_scene and monologue inside the existing shared framework. Feedback should remain grounded in observable acting evidence: text handling, intention, listening, response, eyeline, reader or camera relationship, thought shifts, pacing/rhythm, stillness, physical choices, speech delivery, emotional clarity and suitability to supplied brief or material.\
Acting feedback must be form-specific where the task supports it. Use the most specific supported form or medium: monologue, scene/duologue, screen/self-tape, stage, classical/heightened text, contemporary text or mixed acting task. If the form is unclear, say so and keep language to shared observable Acting domains.\
Finished tapes can show text handling, speech clarity, camera relationship, visible physical choices, reader-mediated response and some acting choices. They must not be used to infer response to direction, group work, workshop behaviour, training potential, interview readiness or broader flexibility unless those capacities are directly shown or the context is explicitly admissions/live-stage.\

\f0\b Feedback specificity rules
\f1\b0 \
Feedback should point to observable beats, text moments, thought shifts, action/intention changes, reaction moments, stakes, speech choices, physical choices, reader/camera relationship and clarity of thought.\
Generic praise or criticism is not sufficient. Terms such as grounded, natural, believable, connected, good eyeline, strong choices, clear relationship, good pacing, emotionally truthful, strong presence, clear character, strong instincts, good voice, confident performance, professional self-tape, good energy and strong text work must be replaced with observable evidence, a timestamp, a text moment, a behavioural choice, a speech choice, a screen/stage task, or an assessability condition.\
If no brief or material context is supplied, do not invent role expectations, production world, given circumstances, accent requirements, casting fit or hidden objectives.\

\f0\b Monologue and text-type handling
\f1\b0 \
In monologue work, evidence should be anchored in the structure of the speech: opening circumstance, changes of thought, shifts in action or address, stakes, rhythm, stillness or physical economy, speech clarity and how the performer handles the text.\
Where the material is classical, Shakespearean, verse or heightened text, comment on text handling, sense, rhythm, imagery, thought progression and speech clarity only where observable. Where the material is contemporary, focus on text-specific choices, conversational rhythm, given circumstances where supplied and behavioural clarity.\

\f0\b Scene / duologue and reader handling
\f1\b0 \
In scene or duologue work, reader evidence is mediated tape evidence. It is valid to comment on the performer\'92s listening, response timing, eyeline, relationship target, thought shifts and use of cues where observable. Reader availability, paid-reader quality, off-camera reader performance or lack of an ideal reader must not be treated as the performer\'92s talent level.\

\f0\b Screen/self-tape task handling
\f1\b0 \
Screen/self-tape Acting evidence must follow the task. Direct-to-camera, off-camera-reader scenes, monologues, ident/slate tasks and read-in-line dialogue use different camera relationships. Do not apply one universal eyeline rule. Comment on eyeline, frame adaptation, non-verbal listening, stillness, physical economy, speech clarity, direct address and relation to camera only where the task makes those relevant and the tape shows them.\

\f0\b Voice/speech and audio
\f1\b0 \
Voice/speech should be labelled and described as speech delivery, not singing, for spoken-only Acting. Speech-delivery evidence may include audibility, clarity, diction, articulation, modulation, pace, pitch, volume, clarity of thought, response to text and suitability to task where observable.\
Separate speech audibility from speech performance. Do not infer weak speech skill from poor recording quality alone. Do not treat accent, speech difference or communication support as a deficit unless the brief explicitly requires a task-specific accent/dialect and the evidence is observable and safe.\

\f0\b Technical / self-tape assessability
\f1\b0 \
Acting self-tape assessment must separate technical assessability from acting quality. Stronger performance claims require readable sight, audible speech, task-appropriate framing, usable eyeline or reader placement, and enough continuity to judge the acting choice. Plain domestic capture, smartphone recording, simple backgrounds and limited props/costume may be fully acceptable if the acting evidence is readable.\
If assessability is materially limited, narrow the claim and lower reliability rather than substituting generic criticism.\

\f0\b Professional presentation boundary
\f1\b0 \
In Acting, professional_presentation should be limited to assessability, brief response, safe process and task preparation that is actually observable. It must not reward expensive equipment, professional readers, paid editing, elaborate sets, cosmetic polish, class-coded presentation, domestic set transformation, marketability, charm, \'93look\'94 or equipment access as acting merit.\

\f0\b Accessibility-safe assessment
\f1\b0 \
Acting assessment must separate access process, communication support and performance evidence. Access needs, visual impairment, neurodivergence, speech difference, mobility difference, interpreters, access workers, line-feeding, accessible scripts, additional preparation time, access riders/passports or adapted audition process must not be treated as acting deficits. Judge only what is shown and assessable in the task.\
Do not infer medical history, capability ceiling, professionalism, confidence or role suitability from access need, disability, speech difference, mobility difference or adaptation.\

\f0\b Methodology-neutrality
\f1\b0 \
Acting feedback should remain methodology-neutral. It may discuss observable intention, action, listening, response, thought shift, physical choice, text handling, stakes, rhythm, camera relationship or speech delivery. It must not require or privilege Stanislavski, Meisner, Method, Brecht, Laban, Lecoq, Viewpoints, Gaulier, Commedia dell\'92Arte or any other named methodology unless the task or brief explicitly asks for it.\

\fs24 \cf0 Acting addendum: form-specific guidance blocks
\f1\b0 \
Use the following as form-specific reference language inside the existing framework:\
\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\ls9\ilvl0\cf0 \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Monologue: text structure, thought shifts, stakes, speech clarity, rhythm, address, stillness/physical economy and progression through the speech.\
\ls9\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Scene / duologue: listening, response timing, relationship target, cue use, reader/camera relationship and thought shifts; reader support remains process support.\
\ls9\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Screen/self-tape: task-specific camera relationship, eyeline, framing, non-verbal listening, stillness, direct address where relevant, and screen-scale physical clarity.\
\ls9\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Stage / heightened text: textual sense, rhythm, imagery, vocal clarity, physical presence and relationship to heightened language where observable.\
\ls9\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Contemporary text: behavioural clarity, conversational rhythm, text-specific stakes and supplied circumstances where available.\
\ls9\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Physical / embodied / fight / creative acting: only where task-present and observable; do not universalise specialist-course evidence.\
\ls9\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Comedy: only where observable through timing, rhythm, contrast, escalation, reaction or text handling; do not create a universal comedy rubric from thin evidence.\
\ls9\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Accessibility / adapted context: treat adaptation and support as process/access context, not an Acting style or deficit.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f0\b \cf0 Acting addendum: live-room-only and process-only claim block
\f1\b0 \
The following should usually be marked as live-room-only, callback-only, admissions-specific or process-only unless directly shown: response to direction, redirection, training potential, workshop behaviour, group work, interview readiness, broader flexibility, access logistics, professional workload handling and reader availability.\

\f0\b Acting addendum: exclusion and anti-bias block
\f1\b0 \
Never use the following as positive or negative Acting scoring evidence unless strictly task-supported and safely observable: singing language in spoken-only acting, body type, appearance, \'93look\'94, protected characteristics, disability or access need as deficit, visual impairment as role-defining unless the brief requires it, neurodivergence as deficit, speech difference as deficit, mobility difference or adaptation as deficit, injury or medical diagnosis from tape, follower count, fame, social-media behaviour, marketability, bookability, expensive production value, studio polish, paid reader availability, reader absence, charm/charisma/presence without observable acting behaviour, class-coded polish, accent hierarchy, method jargon as universal scoring, inferred response to direction, inferred training potential outside admissions/training context, or invented role/world claims without brief support.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 14. Reusable Handoff Pack for ACTING-FINAL-AUDIT\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\fs28 \cf0 ACTING-REV handoff summary\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 The Acting revision is complete at wording and guardrail level. It does not alter the shared six-field architecture, Acting weights, caps, blockers, verdict thresholds, report schema, backend pipeline or Musical Theatre stabilised flow. The revision strengthens the current Acting baseline by making Acting form-specific inside the existing framework, while keeping all score fields intact.\
The most important change is containment of the spoken voice / vocal proxy risk. The revised baseline now states that, although the stored vocal field may continue to operate internally as the speech-delivery slot for Acting, spoken-only reports must not present that category as singing or vocal performance unless singing is actually present. Speech delivery is now framed as audibility, clarity, diction, articulation, modulation, pace, volume and response to text or task, with poor recording quality separated from weak speech performance.\
The second major gain is Acting-form specificity. The revised baseline now distinguishes monologue, scene/duologue, screen/self-tape, stage, classical/heightened text, contemporary text and mixed acting tasks. Screen/self-tape guidance now treats camera relationship and eyeline as task-specific rather than universal. Reader handling is now clearly bounded as process support, not a talent prerequisite.\
The third major gain is claim-scope control. Response to direction, redirection, group work, workshop behaviour, training potential and broader flexibility are now treated as live-room, admissions-specific or process-only evidence unless directly shown. The no-brief guardrail is also strengthened so role, world, production style, hidden objective and casting-fit claims must be grounded in supplied material or observable evidence.\
The fourth major gain is fairness. The revision adds explicit accessibility-safe Acting and speech rules: access needs, visual impairment, neurodivergence, speech difference, mobility difference, support workers, accessible scripts, extra preparation time and adaptation must not be treated as acting deficits. professional_presentation is now bounded to assessability, brief response and safe process, not production polish, paid readers, marketability, appearance or class-coded presentation.\
Residual limitations remain but do not block final audit: frontend labels are unverified, no live Acting outputs were supplied, comedy remains thin, physical/fight evidence is specialist-course-shaped, methodology-neutral descriptor language remains limited, and some access-specific operational detail remains broad.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0
